Census of benchmarks and scripts

Every Python file tracked under benchmarks/ and scripts/ receives one verdict. The population is enumerated through git ls-files, the tracked-file semantics that nova.database.filepath.worktree_path_candidates owns, not a directory walk. A driver known to be tracked must appear or the census refuses. This record was produced by benchmarks/scripts_census.py at revision 615fdd13fd25040b1d3b43a06be7c2585a585617; its machine-readable twin sits at docs/evidence/fragments/code-reuse-and-hot-path/bench-scripts-census.json.

The three verdicts sum to 1574 and the measured population of both trees is 1574, so the counts reconcile: yes.

How a verdict is reached

keep — imported by kept code or tests (nova/, tests/, apps/) or transitively by a kept driver; reached by a live runner (a launcher under scripts/, a test, or the packaging metadata) through a full path, an import statement, or a path assembled from quoted segments; named by a plan in S28–S32, read from that plan's own text rather than guessed from a filename; a package __init__.py; or this census instrument itself.

delete — reached by none of those. Each delete names every evidence record that cites it and the revision that evidence record ran at; the revision is proven to resolve by git cat-file -e <revision>:<path>.

merge — a byte-identical duplicate that folds into a twin classified keep.

A driver cited by a record that names no resolvable revision is undeletable under the locked decision cited-driver-deletion: delete-when-revision-resolves, so the 34 such files are kept and named below rather than deleted on an unresolvable citation.

Controls

Positive control. benchmarks/recovery_ladder_census.py is known to be tracked and must appear in the census, or the census refuses; it is present.

Negative controls. Three forced deletes are refused and named, each for a different reason: benchmarks/recovery_ladder_census.py, a driver a live test imports; scripts/prototypes/stored_state_refusals/panels.py, a neighbour a kept file imports from its own directory; and scripts/iron_linear_magnetisation/gate.py, a file in a directory a focused plan names. Each exits 1 with Refused: delete refused: … is a kept driver (…) and cannot be deleted. Forcing an unkept driver succeeds, so the guard is specific rather than blanket. Each control log records the revision, the command and the exit status.
